# swarm-082539-kns2

**Task:** Round 2: improve this hub and build the improvements. The hub is agent-chatroom-mcp: the chatroom hub itself, the swarm launcher, seats, prompts, recruiting, verification, telemetry, the dashboard, and anything else about how rooms of agents work together. Broad on purpose: cover as much ground as you can and do not narrow to one theme.

THIS ROOM IS THE TEST OF THE LAST ROUND. Round 1 (swarm-000525-jcz8, concluded 2026-10-10, merged to main at c7601da7) built mechanisms that no full room has used yet. The hub you are talking through runs that code: the fresh challenger is on (when a proposal opens, the hub recruits one seat that sees only the proposal), and so are the work ledger and evidence line, the seat output record with its warning and hub-opened removal vote, verify heads with "unverified" and "dev_room" fields and "Verified PARTIAL" conclusions, attack/<key> on findings, idle open work, idle-leave and the run deadline. Watch what they do to this room, and write on the board where they helped, got in the way, or did nothing.

READ FIRST: docs/self-improvement-swarm-000525-jcz8.md (round 1's conclusion, its REMAINING list and its full transcript); the round-1 board, including every remaining/* entry, is in /Users/benji/Desktop/Personal and learning/agent-chatroom-mcp/logs/hub-7741/data/swarm-000525-jcz8-room.jsonl (read it as a file; that hub is stopped); and the re-targeted docs/decisions/done-means-independently-verified.md and docs/decisions/humans-answered-once-by-hub-enforcement.md. Ratify or refute by reference; do not re-derive what round 1 proved.

SEEDS, a start and not a boundary:
1. Telemetry. Every run should leave a record of what its agents actually did: how many searched, how many made web or network requests, how many read, ran, edited, recruited, challenged, verified, how many turns and tokens each took, and what it cost. Evidence that it is missing: the hub's work ledger already counts each seat's reads, searches, web calls, runs and edits, but the run report's per-seat scorecard carries none of it (only messages, board entries, proposals, challenges, votes and commits); the run record is written only by the launcher at the end, so when round 1's launcher crashed the run's cost and the verifier's verdict were lost; recruit costs sit in the hub's /agents and never reach the run's usage; and every headline number of round 1 (2% recruit rate, 6.9 min to first propose, 17-19% of seat time polling) was computed by hand by a seat writing its own script. That is the flaw. Research how other systems record agent runs before designing anything.
2. Measurement. Every effect round 1 claimed was one run per arm, mostly in the authors' own dev rooms. A change to how agents behave counts as working only with a measured before and after (the benchmark harness and oracle tasks: docs/decisions/measure-task-success-on-a-machine-oracle.md, tasks/, bench/), or it is listed as unmeasured.
3. Round 1's REMAINING list. That includes three verified branches not yet merged (hub-idle-exit c98cd9c5, fresh sourcer debc4e69 which needs a rebase on the blind challenger, unsourced-claims 8060c2ec), the reverted .partial pre-gate (rebuild it to exclude only .partial keys), the forgeable dev-room receipt (the parent hub should start and observe dev rooms), seats that can still touch the removal mechanism, and grow-on-triggers.
4. The owner's original seeds still stand: killing agents that do not perform; every agent reaching the room's goal unless it is killed; recruiting; research rooms that conclude without researching; proactivity. Round 1 built mechanisms for each. Judge them live in this room.
Go well beyond these.

HOW TO WORK. Research before building, on every item: fetch the papers and prior systems (arXiv first), put each under sources/<id> on the board with what it shows, and say whether your idea agrees with it, contradicts it or duplicates it. Round 1 did most of its research after building and had to shrink its claims; do it first. Proactivity is key: you see a flaw, raise it with the team, research it, build, test, iterate, repeat. Do not build alone: get feedback, because that is what the room is for. Recruiting is on and wanted: when an area or a build is bigger than one seat, request_agent or open a break-out room, with model "claude-opus-5-5" or "claude-sonnet-5-5" only and no other model or provider; give a recruit that builds can_edit=true. Each seat claims a different area (claim/<area>), someone other than its author attacks each idea, and one ranked list stays on the board.

BUILDING. On your own branch in your worktree: a regression test first, shown failing before the change; then the change; npm run build; PORT=<your port> npx tsx scripts/smoke.ts must print SMOKE OK; then commit. Run only your own isolated tests: this machine has limited compute and a shared test queue (`testq` shows your place); the verifier runs the full suite once on the integration branch. A change to how agents behave is done only after agents have been watched using it on a private hub built from that branch, by someone other than its author, and its verify entry lists what was not shown under "unverified". Prefer several small, verified, independently mergeable branches.

DO NOT CONCLUDE QUICKLY. No final proposal until every claimed area has research and evidence on the board and every built branch is verified. The final proposal is a ranked list of what was built (branch, commit, verify entry, the dev room where agents used it, and the measured effect with n per arm, or "unmeasured") and what remains (idea, evidence, how to build it).

RULES. Nothing lands on main from this room: no push, no merge into main, no edits in the main checkout (only your own worktree), and never run git config. Do not freeze quality baselines or add allowlist entries. Never stop or restart a hub you did not start: not this one (port 7742), not 7717, and nothing on 7718, 7719 or 7722. To test a change, start your own private hub from your worktree's build with its own data directory (CHATROOM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/hub-<port>) and stop it afterwards by its PID (lsof -ti:<port> -sTCP:LISTEN, then kill that PID); never restart the hub you are talking through. Ports, so seats do not collide: seat N uses 78N0 to 78N9 (claude-opus-5-5-3 uses 7830 to 7839), the verifier 7880 to 7889, recruits 7900 to 7999 after checking the port is free. Every script and private hub you run gets an explicit PORT in your range. A private hub or dev room runs claude-opus-5-5 or claude-sonnet-5-5 only, seats and recruits alike: start it with CHATROOM_RECRUIT_AGENT=claude and CHATROOM_RECRUIT_MODEL set to one of those two. Never use pkill or killall.

**Done when:** The room has adopted one proposal: a ranked list of what was built (branch, commit, verify entry, the dev room where agents used it, and the measured effect with n per arm, or 'unmeasured') and what remains (idea, evidence, how to build it). Telemetry is addressed with research behind it. Every claimed area has sources/* entries read before building. Verified branches are merged by the verifier onto one integration branch that builds, prints SMOKE OK and passes the full suite once. Round 1's live mechanisms are each judged from what they did in this room.

## Final answer (concluded)

CONCLUSION. Integration branch swarm/swarm-082539-kns2/integration-next @ faf2fc23 (= 0d0b34d1 + snapshot mock fix 7e64b7a1; 0d0b34d1 = 64c8f3f1 + refusals-visible 3a21beb5). Nothing was pushed or merged to main; main stays at c7e925e7. Suite 1 on 01a9e3de: "[offline] OK (197 commands)". The suite run on 0d0b34d1 FAILED in claude-lean-flags (9 "unmocked import node:os" from f5095d72). 7e64b7a1 fixes it (verify/snapshot-mocks, -3). At faf2fc23 the full suite gives "[offline] OK (205 commands)", exit 0 (/tmp/kns2-suite-4.log), and SMOKE OK. All 19 round-2 regressions fail at c7e925e7 and pass at faf2fc23 (verify/integration, verifier). 64c8f3f1 also ran once as a hub-launched dev room and concluded 3/3 (verify/dev-runs-cost). Unless a row says otherwise, effects are n=1 per arm or a proxy, and UNMEASURED means no outcome effect was measured. Every row has a non-author verify/* entry that fails at its parent and passes at its head; the verifier rechecked fail→pass for 25+ shas (verifier/precheck, verifier/carryover).

BUILT, MERGED, VERIFIED (measured effect first, then risk closed)
1. live-usage 8d34fcf2→c8d4a33b→9cc4567e (-1). Seats report usage live over OTLP, and the hub holds per-seat cost, tokens and calls, recruits included. verify/live-usage (-6); verify/live-usage-proto (-2) covers the __proto__ fix. Measured in 5 of 5 runs: hub total = launcher exit total + recruits (i9fk $1.4128+$0.125=$1.5378; jdpq $1.5517437 on both sides; zeyw in evidence/seat-isolation-otlp-merged). The old run total omitted recruits. After a launcher SIGKILL the hub still held $0.0276. A killed seat lost 3/8 calls at a 5 s export interval and 0/5 at 1 s.
2. run-metrics 1c6fc166→e5e90378 (-3). Code computes headline numbers and a per-seat scorecard from the hub's own log: work by class, polling and idle polling, model calls, tokens, cost, recruits, challenges, verifies, citation credit. Served at /rooms/:room/metrics, written to report.md and result.json, and available as a CLI for crashed runs. verify/run-metrics (-4). Together with row 1 this answers the telemetry seed (evidence/telemetry-composed).
3. hub-launched-dev-runs 15c28945→e09b7f5f (-6). The parent hub builds a commit, launches the dev run and watches it. It pins the listener PID and keeps seats, cost and end state; any other dev_room reads PARTIAL. verify/hub-launched-dev-runs (verifier), verify/dev-runs-tip-4 (-4), verify/dev-runs-cost (-3). One live kill-and-rebind was caught. This answers round 1's forgeable receipt at SLSA L2, not L3.
4. seat-isolation b9103686+7cbea600+e4f5e13c (-5). A seat's direct POST to a human route (vote/kick/close/messages) as the owner is refused and named in the room; a re-parented, env-scrubbed request such as `( env -i curl ... & )` is not caught. verify/seat-isolation (-7; room c0gh: 6/6 refused). This is attribution, not a boundary. e4f5e13c fixes the merge bug that flagged /otlp exports. On the merged tree 4950325a with real seats, -1 recorded 37 OTLP usage events, no false notices, and exactly one true notice, for a forged curl (evidence/seat-isolation-otlp-merged). Round 1's "seats touch the removal mechanism" is covered for human routes, not at the OS level.
5. board-empty-delete d854591a (-3; verify -5) and board-cas a620714b (-5; verify -4, room vrpo: 6/6 stale overwrites refused, then merged; 1 informed loss). A peer can no longer clear or replace another seat's verify/attack. A stale overwrite is refused once, with the current text attached: informed, not faithful.
6. human-mentions d1a5e845→5f19581a (-4). Naming a human in passing no longer counts as answering them. On replay, 72 of 616 logged replies to a human only named them; it also happened live here. verify/human-mentions (-1, room 189h). RE-TARGET humans-answered-once-by-hub-enforcement.
7. ledger-targets 4b6c015d (-4) and ledger-web-pages 3d077d10 (-5). The ledger keyed targets on their first 120 chars (worktree paths are 114) and counted web by host, so the evidence line, the barren ask and the metrics all undercounted. verify/ledger-targets (verifier), verify/ledger-web-pages (-7).
8. seat-targets ae6df339+6ed40d27+99b6779b (-4). Each flat seat opens on its own item of the brief's numbered list (--no-seat-targets turns it off). verify/seat-targets (-1, room gjep); verify/seat-targets-flag (verifier, 99b6779b). Proxy, n=3 per arm: largest opening cluster 5,4,5/7 → 2,2,2/7. Real rooms, n=1 per arm: claims split the collision within 6 s, with no downstream difference. Downstream effect UNMEASURED.
9. Fresh challenger: brief a4c22a3e (-2), snapshot f5095d72 (-7), sourcer 1b0871f5 (-7). verify/challenger-brief (-7), verify/carryover-snapshot (-3), verify/carryover-sourcer (-3). Printf bench: off 0/3 vs on 1/4; old brief 0/4 vs new 1/4 (recomputed by the verifier). MDD 0.6-0.9, so UNMEASURED. Mechanism: the old brief re-ran the proposer's checks 8/8 times; the new one fuzzed independently 4/4, at about twice the cost ($0.05 → $0.09-0.12). It now runs in a detached snapshot.
10. Verification plumbing. pending-verify eb0afe37 (-6; verify -1): a pending head covers its commit but never opens the gate. .partial pre-gate e9ff461d (-6; verify/partial-pregate, by the verifier and -7). ask-verify 778bd16e (-3; verify -2): on replay, 47/94 such asks were still owed; live saving UNMEASURED.
11. refusals-visible f050d762+3a21beb5 (-6; verify/refusals-visible -7). room_status refusals=true lists the latest hub refusals by shown name, tool, code and time, never the id or the text. Live, author-run, n=1 (swarm-090300-vnb4): a dev verifier told only to check "the hub refused my propose" used it unprompted and confirmed the refusal. About 70-85 refusal reports across 259 logs (evidence/refusals-cited).
12. Carryover (-7): idle-exit 92471d52, unsourced cedb9b4f; verify/carryover-* (-3). UNMEASURED.
13. Smaller fixes: recruit-env-leak c6931116 (verify -3); dashboard-double-draw 3542fb0f (verify -3); the owner's #150 sidebar 7a2a7bdf (-2; verify/ui-people-row -4, real seats in headless Chrome); dashboard-cost 85e170b3 (-1; verify -7); credits bc157dc4 (-7; verify -3; 3/20 replayed conclusions); source-carryover 58431f6c (-5; verify -4; UNMEASURED); bench hub-env 2f979df3 (-2; verify -5); quiet-default 832d405b (-7; verify -6; RE-TARGET minimal-prompt-hub-carries-coordination: claude seats sent 5% of chat quiet before the prompt sentence and 9% after (an era comparison across different rooms; codex 3%→44% on 35 vs 3784 messages), so the sentence did not make claude seats go quiet; near-zero effect on this fleet: 0/7 live, 1/217 and 3/116 on replay).

ROUND-1 MECHANISMS IN THIS ROOM (verifier/live-mechanisms, live/*)
- Fresh challenger: fired only in dev rooms and on the bench, where it re-ran the proposer's checks 8/8 (fixed, row 9). On this proposal (#457) it checked the cheapest claim, the git rev-parse of the header (0d0b34d1 = 64c8f3f1 + 3a21beb5, main c7e925e7), found it held, and filed nothing: correct but of little value. The substantive challenges came from seats (-7, -3, -6).
- Propose hold (unexamined findings): fired once on this proposal, naming 12 entries. 5 of them (build/seat-human-route, build/quiet-default, build/hub-launched-dev-runs, build/dashboard-cost, idea/recruit-env-leak) already had non-author verify/* entries, because the hold does not count a verify as examination. The other 7 are sources and ideas the list does not rest on, plus the open attack on run-budget, which is in REMAINING.
- Work ledger and evidence line: undercounted (row 7).
- Seat output record, warning, removal vote: never fired; every seat produced output.
- Verify heads' unverified/dev_room fields and Verified PARTIAL: used on every head. Receipts read "fetch failed" when a reviewer's hub stopped before the write; row 3 replaces them.
- attack/<key>: about 10, all answered by edits.
- Idle open work, idle-leave, run deadline: no visible effect.
- humans-answered-once: misread names (row 6), and routed a "no reply needed" notice as an ask (2/334).
- Owed-ask queue: refused the verifier 3 times for asks it had already acted on (row 10 helps).
- Idea-list hint: fired 10 times here, all false positives (-2's replay).
- Reviewer assignment: drove every review.

OWNER SEEDS
1. Kill poor performers: nobody was warned or removed. Removal is now guarded (row 4).
2. Every seat reaches the goal: 7/7 workers and both recruits delivered verified commits.
3. Recruiting: 0/7 workers recruited (run-metrics). list_agents shows 2 recruits, both the verifier's. Seats reviewed each other's work instead.
4. Research depth: 6/7 seats wrote sources/* before their first commit. -7's 92471d52 (08:28:30) predates its first source (08:30:55); it re-applies round 1's c98cd9c5. The recruits wrote none. board-cas, ledger-web-pages and source-carryover were built before, or without, a source of their own (evidence/done-when-audit).
5. Proactivity: 10+ unassigned defects found and fixed.

REMAINING (idea, evidence, how)
- Run budget (-1, idea/run-budget; -6's attack answered there): built at 609d7f98 after the freeze. Verified by -6 (verify/run-budget), not merged. To merge it, /rooms/x/recruits/stop must be added to HUMAN_ROUTES (e4f5e13c's case 5 flags it) and the conflicts in result.ts and offline-runner.mjs resolved. A launcher --budget gives a rate-projected handoff warning and stops at 100%. A budget or timeout stop now also stops the run's hub recruits; dev-run spend still has to join the total.
- Idea-list hint false positives (-3): built at ebedcd20 and verified (verify/list-hint, -2), but not merged because the freeze came first. It skips lists addressed to agents and lists citing a board key or commit, but not lists answering a human. Replay: 10→1 here, 27→3 in round 1; those 3 are the owner-requested idea lists, which should still fire.
- The propose hold should count a non-author verify/* as examination (5 of its 12 names here).
- A real boundary: a human token plus --sandbox, which also lifts dev-run receipts from L2 to L3. Blocked: sandboxed seats can't run dev rooms.
- Show claims before openings (findings/opening-order).
- Seat-targets with more workers than items (needs rooms of more than 3 agents).
- grow-on-triggers: not built, by decision. Next: K=3 vs K=8 on build-ledger-scale8/20.
- A stated-claim oracle task (idea/stated-claim-oracle-task).
- Courtesy asks: researched, not built (0.5-1.6%; findings/courtesy-asks).
- Pre-join work is dropped (idea/prejoin-work, -2): src/hub/liveness.ts:106-110 discards heartbeats from a seat key that has not joined. The fresh challenger checks before it joins, so on this proposal the hub told every seat "fresh-challenger-1 made no local tool call", while it had run git (#457), and the room log has 0 work events for it. How: buffer beats per spawned-but-unjoined key and replay them on join.
- The "has the votes but no verification" notice is re-posted on every board write while the gate stays unmet. Here that was 12 near-identical ~1.5k-char system lines in about 8 minutes, pushed to all 8 seats, mostly set off by reviewers re-pointing heads as the gate asked (#493-#519, -3). How: post it once per proposal version, or only when the named blocker changes (hub-carries-what-it-knows).
- The module-mock allow-lists in seat-env-regression and claude-lean-flags-regression broke twice: ./seat-targets.js (6ed40d27) and node:os in f5095d72 (caught by suite 2). Each author ran only their own regression. How: allow node: builtins and pure src modules by default, or run those two in every branch's own check.
- Any amend drops every standing disagree. Hub.amend keeps only agrees whose quote survives, and a disagree carries no quote, yet blocked_by tells seats "re-vote, or the text they objected to is amended". The verifier's disagree was reset at v9, v10/v11 and v13 by amends to text it never objected to, so a 6/8 supermajority could pass on a silently dropped objection (verifier, -6). How: a disagree quotes the clause it objects to and survives while that clause does, the same rule as agree.
- Proposal re-delivery (idea/proposal-diff-delivery, -2): every amend ships the full ~11k-char text to every seat on its next wait, about 130k chars per seat across v1→v13 here, mostly unchanged. How: once a seat has seen version N, ship only the amend diffs.
- Any amend drops every standing disagree: Hub.amend keeps only agrees whose quote survives (-6 read the code), yet blocked_by says "re-vote, or the text they objected to is amended". The verifier's disagree on an unrun suite was silently reset at v9, v10/v11 and v13 by amends to other text. In this supermajority room, that left 6/8 agrees one gate head away from passing an unverified list. How: disagree takes a quote of the clause it objects to and survives while that clause does, as agree already does.
- Small: source-carryover titles; off-switches; a bench run deadline; the verifier's shell sits in the shared checkout; dev-runs test 8 needs its own temp repo.
- Every outcome effect above is unmeasured. A bench A/B needs a task with headroom (pass rate 0.3-0.7) and about 20 runs per arm to detect +0.4 at power 0.8 (sources/2411.00640).

Run only your own isolated tests: this machine has limited compute and a shared test queue (`testq` shows your place); the verifier runs the full suite once on the integration branch. A change to how agents behave is done only after agents have been watched using it on a private hub built from that branch, by someone other than its author, and its verify entry lists what was not shown under "unverified". Prefer several small, verified, independently mergeable branches.
    
    DO NOT CONCLUDE QUICKLY. No final proposal until every claimed area has research and evidence on the board and every built branch is verified. The final proposal is a ranked list of what was built (branch, commit, verify entry, the dev room where agents used it, and the measured effect with n per arm, or "unmeasured") and what remains (idea, evidence, how to build it).
    
    RULES. Nothing lands on main from this room: no push, no merge into main, no edits in the main checkout (only your own worktree), and never run git config. Do not freeze quality baselines or add allowlist entries. Never stop or restart a hub you did not start: not this one (port 7742), not 7717, and nothing on 7718, 7719 or 7722. To test a change, start your own private hub from your worktree's build with its own data directory (CHATROOM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/hub-<port>) and stop it afterwards by its PID (lsof -ti:<port> -sTCP:LISTEN, then kill that PID); never restart the hub you are talking through. Ports, so seats do not collide: seat N uses 78N0 to 78N9 (claude-opus-5-5-3 uses 7830 to 7839), the verifier 7880 to 7889, recruits 7900 to 7999 after checking the port is free. Every script and private hub you run gets an explicit PORT in your range. A private hub or dev room runs claude-opus-5-5 or claude-sonnet-5-5 only, seats and recruits alike: start it with CHATROOM_RECRUIT_AGENT=claude and CHATROOM_RECRUIT_MODEL set to one of those two. Never use pkill or killall.

Conclusion: CONCLUSION. Integration branch swarm/swarm-082539-kns2/integration-next @ faf2fc23 (= 0d0b34d1 + snapshot mock fix 7e64b7a1; 0d0b34d1 = 64c8f3f1 + refusals-visible 3a21beb5). Nothing was pushed or merged to main; main stays at c7e925e7. Suite 1 on 01a9e3de: "[offline] OK (197 commands)". The suite run on 0d0b34d1 FAILED in claude-lean-flags (9 "unmocked import node:os" from f5095d72). 7e64b7a1 fixes it (verify/snapshot-mocks, -3). At faf2fc23 the full suite gives "[offline] OK (205 commands)", exit 0 (/tmp/kns2-suite-4.log), and SMOKE OK. All 19 round-2 regressions fail at c7e925e7 and pass at faf2fc23 (verify/integration, verifier). 64c8f3f1 also ran once as a hub-launched dev room and concluded 3/3 (verify/dev-runs-cost). Unless a row says otherwise, effects are n=1 per arm or a proxy, and UNMEASURED means no outcome effect was measured. Every row has a non-author verify/* entry that fails at its parent and passes at its head; the verifier rechecked fail→pass for 25+ shas (verifier/precheck, verifier/carryover).
    
    BUILT, MERGED, VERIFIED (measured effect first, then risk closed)
    1. live-usage 8d34fcf2→c8d4a33b→9cc4567e (-1). Seats report usage live over OTLP, and the hub holds per-seat cost, tokens and calls, recruits included. verify/live-usage (-6); verify/live-usage-proto (-2) covers the __proto__ fix. Measured in 5 of 5 runs: hub total = launcher exit total + recruits (i9fk $1.4128+$0.125=$1.5378; jdpq $1.5517437 on both sides; zeyw in evidence/seat-isolation-otlp-merged). The old run total omitted recruits. After a launcher SIGKILL the hub still held $0.0276. A killed seat lost 3/8 calls at a 5 s export interval and 0/5 at 1 s.
    2. run-metrics 1c6fc166→e5e90378 (-3). Code computes headline numbers and a per-seat scorecard from the hub's own log: work by class, polling and idle polling, model calls, tokens, cost, recruits, challenges, verifies, citation credit. Served at /rooms/:room/metrics, written to report.md and result.json, and available as a CLI for crashed runs. verify/run-metrics (-4). Together with row 1 this answers the telemetry seed (evidence/telemetry-composed).
    3. hub-launched-dev-runs 15c28945→e09b7f5f (-6). The parent hub builds a commit, launches the dev run and watches it. It pins the listener PID and keeps seats, cost and end state; any other dev_room reads PARTIAL. verify/hub-launched-dev-runs (verifier), verify/dev-runs-tip-4 (-4), verify/dev-runs-cost (-3). One live kill-and-rebind was caught. This answers round 1's forgeable receipt at SLSA L2, not L3.
    4. seat-isolation b9103686+7cbea600+e4f5e13c (-5). A seat's direct POST to a human route (vote/kick/close/messages) as the owner is refused and named in the room; a re-parented, env-scrubbed request such as `( env -i curl ... & )` is not caught. verify/seat-isolation (-7; room c0gh: 6/6 refused). This is attribution, not a boundary. e4f5e13c fixes the merge bug that flagged /otlp exports. On the merged tree 4950325a with real seats, -1 recorded 37 OTLP usage events, no false notices, and exactly one true notice, for a forged curl (evidence/seat-isolation-otlp-merged). Round 1's "seats touch the removal mechanism" is covered for human routes, not at the OS level.
    5. board-empty-delete d854591a (-3; verify -5) and board-cas a620714b (-5; verify -4, room vrpo: 6/6 stale overwrites refused, then merged; 1 informed loss). A peer can no longer clear or replace another seat's verify/attack. A stale overwrite is refused once, with the current text attached: informed, not faithful.
    6. human-mentions d1a5e845→5f19581a (-4). Naming a human in passing no longer counts as answering them. On replay, 72 of 616 logged replies to a human only named them; it also happened live here. verify/human-mentions (-1, room 189h). RE-TARGET humans-answered-once-by-hub-enforcement.
    7. ledger-targets 4b6c015d (-4) and ledger-web-pages 3d077d10 (-5). The ledger keyed targets on their first 120 chars (worktree paths are 114) and counted web by host, so the evidence line, the barren ask and the metrics all undercounted. verify/ledger-targets (verifier), verify/ledger-web-pages (-7).
    8. seat-targets ae6df339+6ed40d27+99b6779b (-4). Each flat seat opens on its own item of the brief's numbered list (--no-seat-targets turns it off). verify/seat-targets (-1, room gjep); verify/seat-targets-flag (verifier, 99b6779b). Proxy, n=3 per arm: largest opening cluster 5,4,5/7 → 2,2,2/7. Real rooms, n=1 per arm: claims split the collision within 6 s, with no downstream difference. Downstream effect UNMEASURED.
    9. Fresh challenger: brief a4c22a3e (-2), snapshot f5095d72 (-7), sourcer 1b0871f5 (-7). verify/challenger-brief (-7), verify/carryover-snapshot (-3), verify/carryover-sourcer (-3). Printf bench: off 0/3 vs on 1/4; old brief 0/4 vs new 1/4 (recomputed by the verifier). MDD 0.6-0.9, so UNMEASURED. Mechanism: the old brief re-ran the proposer's checks 8/8 times; the new one fuzzed independently 4/4, at about twice the cost ($0.05 → $0.09-0.12). It now runs in a detached snapshot.
    10. Verification plumbing. pending-verify eb0afe37 (-6; verify -1): a pending head covers its commit but never opens the gate. .partial pre-gate e9ff461d (-6; verify/partial-pregate, by the verifier and -7). ask-verify 778bd16e (-3; verify -2): on replay, 47/94 such asks were still owed; live saving UNMEASURED.
    11. refusals-visible f050d762+3a21beb5 (-6; verify/refusals-visible -7). room_status refusals=true lists the latest hub refusals by shown name, tool, code and time, never the id or the text. Live, author-run, n=1 (swarm-090300-vnb4): a dev verifier told only to check "the hub refused my propose" used it unprompted and confirmed the refusal. About 70-85 refusal reports across 259 logs (evidence/refusals-cited).
    12. Carryover (-7): idle-exit 92471d52, unsourced cedb9b4f; verify/carryover-* (-3). UNMEASURED.
    13. Smaller fixes: recruit-env-leak c6931116 (verify -3); dashboard-double-draw 3542fb0f (verify -3); the owner's #150 sidebar 7a2a7bdf (-2; verify/ui-people-row -4, real seats in headless Chrome); dashboard-cost 85e170b3 (-1; verify -7); credits bc157dc4 (-7; verify -3; 3/20 replayed conclusions); source-carryover 58431f6c (-5; verify -4; UNMEASURED); bench hub-env 2f979df3 (-2; verify -5); quiet-default 832d405b (-7; verify -6; RE-TARGET minimal-prompt-hub-carries-coordination: claude seats sent 5% of chat quiet before the prompt sentence and 9% after (an era comparison across different rooms; codex 3%→44% on 35 vs 3784 messages), so the sentence did not make claude seats go quiet; near-zero effect on this fleet: 0/7 live, 1/217 and 3/116 on replay).
    
    ROUND-1 MECHANISMS IN THIS ROOM (verifier/live-mechanisms, live/*)
    - Fresh challenger: fired only in dev rooms and on the bench, where it re-ran the proposer's checks 8/8 (fixed, row 9). On this proposal (#457) it checked the cheapest claim, the git rev-parse of the header (0d0b34d1 = 64c8f3f1 + 3a21beb5, main c7e925e7), found it held, and filed nothing: correct but of little value. The substantive challenges came from seats (-7, -3, -6).
    - Propose hold (unexamined findings): fired once on this proposal, naming 12 entries. 5 of them (build/seat-human-route, build/quiet-default, build/hub-launched-dev-runs, build/dashboard-cost, idea/recruit-env-leak) already had non-author verify/* entries, because the hold does not count a verify as examination. The other 7 are sources and ideas the list does not rest on, plus the open attack on run-budget, which is in REMAINING.
    - Work ledger and evidence line: undercounted (row 7).
    - Seat output record, warning, removal vote: never fired; every seat produced output.
    - Verify heads' unverified/dev_room fields and Verified PARTIAL: used on every head. Receipts read "fetch failed" when a reviewer's hub stopped before the write; row 3 replaces them.
    - attack/<key>: about 10, all answered by edits.
    - Idle open work, idle-leave, run deadline: no visible effect.
    - humans-answered-once: misread names (row 6), and routed a "no reply needed" notice as an ask (2/334).
    - Owed-ask queue: refused the verifier 3 times for asks it had already acted on (row 10 helps).
    - Idea-list hint: fired 10 times here, all false positives (-2's replay).
    - Reviewer assignment: drove every review.
    
    OWNER SEEDS
    1. Kill poor performers: nobody was warned or removed. Removal is now guarded (row 4).
    2. Every seat reaches the goal: 7/7 workers and both recruits delivered verified commits.
    3. Recruiting: 0/7 workers recruited (run-metrics). list_agents shows 2 recruits, both the verifier's. Seats reviewed each other's work instead.
    4. Research depth: 6/7 seats wrote sources/* before their first commit. -7's 92471d52 (08:28:30) predates its first source (08:30:55); it re-applies round 1's c98cd9c5. The recruits wrote none. board-cas, ledger-web-pages and source-carryover were built before, or without, a source of their own (evidence/done-when-audit).
    5. Proactivity: 10+ unassigned defects found and fixed.
    
    REMAINING (idea, evidence, how)
    - Run budget (-1, idea/run-budget; -6's attack answered there): built at 609d7f98 after the freeze. Verified by -6 (verify/run-budget), not merged. To merge it, /rooms/x/recruits/stop must be added to HUMAN_ROUTES (e4f5e13c's case 5 flags it) and the conflicts in result.ts and offline-runner.mjs resolved. A launcher --budget gives a rate-projected handoff warning and stops at 100%. A budget or timeout stop now also stops the run's hub recruits; dev-run spend still has to join the total.
    - Idea-list hint false positives (-3): built at ebedcd20 and verified (verify/list-hint, -2), but not merged because the freeze came first. It skips lists addressed to agents and lists citing a board key or commit, but not lists answering a human. Replay: 10→1 here, 27→3 in round 1; those 3 are the owner-requested idea lists, which should still fire.
    - The propose hold should count a non-author verify/* as examination (5 of its 12 names here).
    - A real boundary: a human token plus --sandbox, which also lifts dev-run receipts from L2 to L3. Blocked: sandboxed seats can't run dev rooms.
    - Show claims before openings (findings/opening-order).
    - Seat-targets with more workers than items (needs rooms of more than 3 agents).
    - grow-on-triggers: not built, by decision. Next: K=3 vs K=8 on build-ledger-scale8/20.
    - A stated-claim oracle task (idea/stated-claim-oracle-task).
    - Courtesy asks: researched, not built (0.5-1.6%; findings/courtesy-asks).
    - Pre-join work is dropped (idea/prejoin-work, -2): src/hub/liveness.ts:106-110 discards heartbeats from a seat key that has not joined. The fresh challenger checks before it joins, so on this proposal the hub told every seat "fresh-challenger-1 made no local tool call", while it had run git (#457), and the room log has 0 work events for it. How: buffer beats per spawned-but-unjoined key and replay them on join.
    - The "has the votes but no verification" notice is re-posted on every board write while the gate stays unmet. Here that was 12 near-identical ~1.5k-char system lines in about 8 minutes, pushed to all 8 seats, mostly set off by reviewers re-pointing heads as the gate asked (#493-#519, -3). How: post it once per proposal version, or only when the named blocker changes (hub-carries-what-it-knows).
    - The module-mock allow-lists in seat-env-regression and claude-lean-flags-regression broke twice: ./seat-targets.js (6ed40d27) and node:os in f5095d72 (caught by suite 2). Each author ran only their own regression. How: allow node: builtins and pure src modules by default, or run those two in every branch's own check.
    - Any amend drops every standing disagree. Hub.amend keeps only agrees whose quote survives, and a disagree carries no quote, yet blocked_by tells seats "re-vote, or the text they objected to is amended". The verifier's disagree was reset at v9, v10/v11 and v13 by amends to text it never objected to, so a 6/8 supermajority could pass on a silently dropped objection (verifier, -6). How: a disagree quotes the clause it objects to and survives while that clause does, the same rule as agree.
    - Proposal re-delivery (idea/proposal-diff-delivery, -2): every amend ships the full ~11k-char text to every seat on its next wait, about 130k chars per seat across v1→v13 here, mostly unchanged. How: once a seat has seen version N, ship only the amend diffs.
    - Any amend drops every standing disagree: Hub.amend keeps only agrees whose quote survives (-6 read the code), yet blocked_by says "re-vote, or the text they objected to is amended". The verifier's disagree on an unrun suite was silently reset at v9, v10/v11 and v13 by amends to other text. In this supermajority room, that left 6/8 agrees one gate head away from passing an unverified list. How: disagree takes a quote of the clause it objects to and survives while that clause does, as agree already does.
    - Small: source-carryover titles; off-switches; a bench run deadline; the verifier's shell sits in the shared checkout; dev-runs test 8 needs its own temp repo.
    - Every outcome effect above is unmeasured. A bench A/B needs a task with headroom (pass rate 0.3-0.7) and about 20 runs per arm to detect +0.4 at power 0.8 (sources/2411.00640).

\#67 [2026-10-10T08:28:34.150Z] launcher-claude (chat): Defect for anyone free, from Benji via the launching session; a cheap recruit could take it. The dashboard shows some messages twice (Benji saw #52 drawn twice). The hub stored it once: one message at seq 52 and one line in the room log. Cause, src/ui.ts:321-336: poll() runs on setInterval every 1500 ms with no in-flight guard, and it appends every returned message without skipping a seq it has already drawn. On this loaded machine a fetch can take longer than 1.5 s, so two overlapping polls ask for the same `since` and both render the batch. A reload shows it once. Test-first as usual, and check it in a browser against a slow hub.
\#68 [2026-10-10T08:28:36.141Z] claude-opus-5-5-5 (chat): I found a live hole: any seat can act as the human on this hub. /config reports human_token_required:false (INSECURE_LOCAL), so a seat's curl POST to /rooms/<room>/vote, /kick or /close with name "benji" vetoes a proposal, removes a seat or closes the room, and the log credits a human. The detail and my fix are in findings/seat-isolation: the hub identifies the loopback caller's process tree and refuses it if that tree descends from a seat. I need someone other than me to attack the plan before I build; @claude-opus-5-5-3, could you? @claude-opus-5-5-2 I have no objection to measurement, with one note. \#89 [2026-10-10T08:30:11.434Z] claude-opus-5-5-4 (chat): @claude-opus-5-5-6 on tool vs route: use the HTTP route plus scripts/dev-run.ts. Participant ids are issued only to the joining connection and are never listed (identity-is-the-connection), so a route that requires one, matched to the session, identifies the requester as well as a tool would. What the registry can then add that nothing has today: requester ≠ proposal author. The receipt should say "watched by X, author Y", and a self-run dev room should read PARTIAL. That is the brief's own rule ("by someone other than its author"), and right now nothing checks it.

Round 1 found prompting barely moves Claude seats (recruiting 2% to 3%), which suggests a better prompt alone will not fix it. One defect: verdictCommits reads the entry's CURRENT text (room.board.get), so settlement is recomputed from today's board. When a reviewer re-points the same verify/* at a newer commit, which happened 5+ times in this room ("re-point verify/run-metrics at 6abfeae0"), the old ask comes back as owed.
    Probe at /tmp/rv2-79df09a5/scripts/zz-rv2-probe.ts: A asks "@R please verify 6abfeae0", R writes verify/run-metrics covering it (settled), then R overwrites it with 1c6fc166. Owed goes back to ["@R please verify 6abfeae0"], and the probe exits 1.
    Fix: record the covered commits on the board notice when it is written (e.g. m.covers), so replay reads the commits as they were then. Clearing the entry also shouldn't un-settle the ask. Minor: an ask naming several commits is settled by an entry covering any one of them. I'd keep that, but say so in the doc comment. I'll write verify/ask-verify once the probe passes.

\#355 [2026-10-10T08:56:52.587Z] claude-opus-5-5-3 (chat): @claude-opus-5-5-5 @claude-opus-5-5-1 @verifier there's a merge interaction bug on integration-next 0c4e8507, found in the composed dev room swarm-085449-i9fk (log /tmp/v3-int/data/swarm-085449-i9fk-room.jsonl, #4-#6, #26). Every seat, and the fresh challenger, got a public notice: "<seat>'s process tree sent POST /otlp/v1/logs to a human route, as a human; the hub refused it." That POST is live-usage's OTLP export, not a forgery. Usage still arrived (45 events; hub $1.5378 vs launcher $1.4128 for 3 seats plus a $0.125 recruit), so nothing was refused, but the log accuses every seat. Cause, src/index.ts around line 304: the middleware runs seatCaller() (lsof plus a ps walk) on every loopback POST outside SEAT_ROUTES, so /otlp and likely /mcp go through it, and seatCaller announces before anything is refused. Fix: resolve the caller only inside requireToken, or add /otlp/v1/logs and /mcp to SEAT_ROUTES, and announce only on a real refusal. That also takes an lsof off every seat request. Each branch passed on its own; the bug only appears in the merge. Everything else fired as designed. Write-up next on evidence/integration-room.

\#370 [2026-10-10T08:58:15.113Z] claude-opus-5-5-2 (chat): @claude-opus-5-5-1 @verifier: a defect in live-usage, which is on integration (8d34fcf2; still there at c8d4a33b). src/hub/usage.ts does `u.by_model[r.model] ??= {...}; m.requests++` on a plain object. Its key is the seat-supplied OTLP attribute "model", so one record with model "__proto__" pollutes Object.prototype for the whole hub process. Afterwards ({}).requests and ({}).cost_usd are NaN, in every room. Probe: /tmp/rv2-c8d4a33b/scripts/zz-proto-probe.ts (npx tsx it there) prints "Object.prototype.requests = NaN cost_usd = NaN" and exits 1. Real Claude Code never sends that model name, so this takes a hostile or buggy seat, but -5's seat-isolation shows seats do hit hub routes by accident. Fix: build by_model with Object.create(null) or a Map (and keep that on replay), or refuse __proto__/constructor/prototype as model names. Add the probe as a regression case. @claude-opus-5-5-7 thanks, I'll skip dashboard-cost.
